Tahukah Kamu?

Setelah Nabi Muhammad ﷺ wafat, siapa yang melanjutkan kepemimpinan umat Islam? Bagaimana cara mereka memimpin? Dan apa jasa-jasa mereka untuk dunia?

Hubungan dengan Kehidupanmu

Di sekolah, kamu punya ketua kelas yang memimpin teman-teman. Bayangkan jika tidak ada pemimpin, apa yang terjadi? Begitu juga dengan umat Islam setelah Nabi wafat.

Pertanyaan untuk Dipikirkan

Siapakah Khulafaurrasyidin itu? Mengapa mereka disebut "yang mendapat petunjuk"?

{showAnswer && (

Khulafaurrasyidin artinya "para pemimpin yang mendapat petunjuk". Mereka adalah 4 khalifah pertama setelah Nabi Muhammad ﷺ wafat: Abu Bakar, Umar, Utsman, dan Ali. Pada bab ini kita akan mempelajari Abu Bakar al-Ṣiddiq dan Umar bin Khattab.

Khulafaurrasyidin (خلفاء الراشدين) artinya "para pemimpin yang mendapat petunjuk Allah". Mereka adalah empat sahabat Nabi yang menjadi pemimpin umat Islam setelah Nabi Muhammad ﷺ wafat.

📋 Urutan Khulafaurrasyidin:

  1. Abu Bakar al-Ṣiddiq (632-634 M)
  2. Umar bin Khattab (634-644 M)
  3. Utsman bin Affan (644-656 M)
  4. Ali bin Abi Thalib (656-661 M)

💡 Ingat: Di bab ini, kita fokus mempelajari Abu Bakar al-Ṣiddiq dan Umar bin Khattab.

) }, { title: 'Abu Bakar al-Ṣiddiq', icon: '👤', content: (

📜 Nasab & Gelar

  • • Nama lengkap: Abdullah bin Abi Quhafah (Utsman) bin Amir
  • • Nama sebelum Islam: Abdul Ka'bah
  • • Gelar: Abu Bakar (bapak orang yang bertaubat) & al-Ṣiddiq (yang membenarkan)
  • • Mengapa disebut al-Ṣiddiq? Karena beliau segera membenarkan peristiwa Isra' Mi'raj ketika orang lain ragu
  • • Lahir: 573 M di Mekah
  • • Suku: Quraisy, Bani Taim

📢 Dakwah

  • • Orang pertama dari kalangan laki-laki dewasa yang masuk Islam
  • • Mendakwahkan Islam dengan lemah lembut dan bijaksana
  • • Menemani Nabi Muhammad ﷺ saat hijrah ke Madinah
  • • Menjadi imam shalat ketika Nabi sakit

⭐ Jasa-jasa

  • • Memerangi orang murtad - mempertahankan keutuhan Islam
  • • Memerangi orang enggan bayar zakat - menegakkan hukum Islam
  • • Mengumpulkan kodeks Al-Qur'an - atas usulan Umar bin Khattab
  • • Mengirim pasukan usamah - melindungi perbatasan
  • • Memperluas wilayah dakwah ke Persia dan Romawi
) }, { title: 'Umar bin Khattab', icon: '👤', content: (

📜 Nasab & Gelar

  • • Nama lengkap: Umar bin Khattab bin Nufail
  • • Gelar: al-Faruq (pembeda antara hak dan batil)
  • • Mengapa disebut al-Faruq? Karena Islam menjadi kuat dan terang-terangan sejak Umar masuk Islam
  • • Lahir: 584 M di Mekah
  • • Suku: Quraisy, Bani Adi
  • • Sebelum Islam: Sempat memusuhi Islam, lalu masuk Islam tahun 6 H

📢 Dakwah

  • • Masuk Islam membuat umat Islam bisa beribadah secara terbuka
  • • Dikenal tegas, adil, dan berani dalam menegakkan kebenaran
  • • Menjadi penasehat terpercaya Nabi Muhammad ﷺ
  • • Dijabat sebagai khalifah kedua setelah Abu Bakar wafat (634 M)

⭐ Jasa-jasa

  • • Membentuk Baitul Mal (kas negara) untuk mengelola keuangan
  • • Membentuk jawatan pos untuk komunikasi antar wilayah
  • • Membuat kalender Hijriyah - dihitung dari peristiwa hijrah
  • • Memperluas wilayah Islam - Persia, Syam, Mesir, Palestina
  • • Mendirikan lembaga peradilan (qadhi/hakim)
  • • Menata administrasi negara dan membagi wilayah menjadi provinsi
  • • Membangun kota-kota baru seperti Kufah, Basrah, dan Fustat
) }, { title: 'Keteladanan', icon: '💎', content: (

🌟 Sikap Teladan dari Abu Bakar:

  • • Lemah lembut dalam berdakwah
  • • Berani mempertahankan kebenaran
  • • Sederhana despite being a leader

🌟 Sikap Teladan dari Umar:

  • • Tegas dan adil dalam mengambil keputusan

  • • Sederhana - blusukan mengecek rakyatnya

Is it OK to rebase it? # Does the topic really exist? git show-ref -q "$topic" || { echo >&2 "No such branch $topic" exit 1 } # Is topic fully merged to master? not_in_master=`git rev-list --pretty=oneline ^master "$topic"` if test -z "$not_in_master" then echo >&2 "$topic is fully merged to master; better remove it." exit 1 ;# we could allow it, but there is no point. fi # Is topic ever merged to next? If so you should not be rebasing it. only_next_1=`git rev-list ^master "^$topic" ${publish} | sort` only_next_2=`git rev-list ^master ${publish} | sort` if test "$only_next_1" = "$only_next_2" then not_in_topic=`git rev-list "^$topic" master` if test -z "$not_in_topic" then echo >&2 "$topic is already up to date with master" exit 1 ;# we could allow it, but there is no point. else exit 0 fi else not_in_next=`git rev-list --pretty=oneline ^${publish} "$topic"` /usr/bin/perl -e ' my $topic = $ARGV[0]; my $msg = "* $topic has commits already merged to public branch:\n"; my (%not_in_next) = map { /^([0-9a-f]+) /; ($1 => 1); } split(/\n/, $ARGV[1]); for my $elem (map { /^([0-9a-f]+) (.*)$/; [$1 => $2]; } split(/\n/, $ARGV[2])) { if (!exists $not_in_next{$elem->[0]}) { if ($msg) { print STDERR $msg; undef $msg; } print STDERR " $elem->[1]\n"; } } ' "$topic" "$not_in_next" "$not_in_master" exit 1 fi <<\DOC_END This sample hook safeguards topic branches that have been published from being rewound. The workflow assumed here is: * Once a topic branch forks from "master", "master" is never merged into it again (either directly or indirectly). * Once a topic branch is fully cooked and merged into "master", it is deleted. If you need to build on top of it to correct earlier mistakes, a new topic branch is created by forking at the tip of the "master". This is not strictly necessary, but it makes it easier to keep your history simple. * Whenever you need to test or publish your changes to topic branches, merge them into "next" branch. The script, being an example, hardcodes the publish branch name to be "next", but it is trivial to make it configurable via $GIT_DIR/config mechanism. With this workflow, you would want to know: (1) ... if a topic branch has ever been merged to "next". Young topic branches can have stupid mistakes you would rather clean up before publishing, and things that have not been merged into other branches can be easily rebased without affecting other people. But once it is published, you would not want to rewind it. (2) ... if a topic branch has been fully merged to "master". Then you can delete it. More importantly, you should not build on top of it -- other people may already want to change things related to the topic as patches against your "master", so if you need further changes, it is better to fork the topic (perhaps with the same name) afresh from the tip of "master". Let's look at this example: o---o---o---o---o---o---o---o---o---o "next" / / / / / a---a---b A / / / / / / / / c---c---c---c B / / / / \ / / / / b---b C \ / / / / / \ / ---o---o---o---o---o---o---o---o---o---o---o "master" A, B and C are topic branches. * A has one fix since it was merged up to "next". * B has finished. It has been fully merged up to "master" and "next", and is ready to be deleted. * C has not merged to "next" at all. We would want to allow C to be rebased, refuse A, and encourage B to be deleted. To compute (1): git rev-list ^master ^topic next git rev-list ^master next if these match, topic has not merged in next at all. To compute (2): git rev-list master..topic if this is empty, it is fully merged to "master".
